# Detailed CFL Solver Benchmark Results

Per-module measurements from [`full_combined.json`](full_combined.json): 20 programs, 2685 modules, two grammars.

- **Time** — total CFL algorithm runtime per module, in seconds.
- **Peak memory** — peak RSS per module, in GB.
- All modules are included (no time filter).
- `—` — no measurement for that (solver, module) pair.
- Bold rows — geometric mean over the benchmark's modules (computed over positive values).
